+#ifdef CONFIG_KSU_SUSFS
+static int susfs_selinux_setprocattr(const char *name, void *value, size_t size)
+{
+	struct task_security_struct *tsec;
+	struct cred *new;
+	u32 mysid = current_sid(), sid = 0, ptsid;
+	int error;
+	char *str = value;
+
+	/*
+	 * Basic control over ability to set these attributes at all.
+	 */
+	if (!strcmp(name, "exec"))
+		error = avc_has_perm(&selinux_state,
+				     mysid, mysid, SECCLASS_PROCESS,
+				     PROCESS__SETEXEC, NULL);
+	else if (!strcmp(name, "fscreate"))
+		error = avc_has_perm(&selinux_state,
+				     mysid, mysid, SECCLASS_PROCESS,
+				     PROCESS__SETFSCREATE, NULL);
+	else if (!strcmp(name, "keycreate"))
+		error = avc_has_perm(&selinux_state,
+				     mysid, mysid, SECCLASS_PROCESS,
+				     PROCESS__SETKEYCREATE, NULL);
+	else if (!strcmp(name, "sockcreate"))
+		error = avc_has_perm(&selinux_state,
+				     mysid, mysid, SECCLASS_PROCESS,
+				     PROCESS__SETSOCKCREATE, NULL);
+	else if (!strcmp(name, "current"))
+		error = avc_has_perm(&selinux_state,
+				     mysid, mysid, SECCLASS_PROCESS,
+				     PROCESS__SETCURRENT, NULL);
+	else
+		error = -EINVAL;
+	if (error)
+		return error;
+
+	/* Obtain a SID for the context, if one was specified. */
+	if (size && str[0] && str[0] != '\n') {
+		if (str[size-1] == '\n') {
+			str[size-1] = 0;
+			size--;
+		}
+		error = susfs_security_context_to_sid(&selinux_state, value, size,
+						&sid, GFP_KERNEL);
+		if (error == -EINVAL && !strcmp(name, "fscreate")) {
+			if (!has_cap_mac_admin(true)) {
+				struct audit_buffer *ab;
+				size_t audit_size;
+
+				/* We strip a nul only if it is at the end, otherwise the
+				 * context contains a nul and we should audit that */
+				if (str[size - 1] == '\0')
+					audit_size = size - 1;
+				else
+					audit_size = size;
+				ab = audit_log_start(audit_context(),
+						     GFP_ATOMIC,
+						     AUDIT_SELINUX_ERR);
+				if (!ab)
+					return error;
+				audit_log_format(ab, "op=fscreate invalid_context=");
+				audit_log_n_untrustedstring(ab, value, audit_size);
+				audit_log_end(ab);
+
+				return error;
+			}
+			error = security_context_to_sid_force(
+						      &selinux_state,
+						      value, size, &sid);
+		}
+		if (error)
+			return error;
+	}
+
+	new = prepare_creds();
+	if (!new)
+		return -ENOMEM;
+
+	/* Permission checking based on the specified context is
+	   performed during the actual operation (execve,
+	   open/mkdir/...), when we know the full context of the
+	   operation.  See selinux_bprm_creds_for_exec for the execve
+	   checks and may_create for the file creation checks. The
+	   operation will then fail if the context is not permitted. */
+	tsec = selinux_cred(new);
+	if (!strcmp(name, "exec")) {
+		tsec->exec_sid = sid;
+	} else if (!strcmp(name, "fscreate")) {
+		tsec->create_sid = sid;
+	} else if (!strcmp(name, "keycreate")) {
+		if (sid) {
+			error = avc_has_perm(&selinux_state, mysid, sid,
+					     SECCLASS_KEY, KEY__CREATE, NULL);
+			if (error)
+				goto abort_change;
+		}
+		tsec->keycreate_sid = sid;
+	} else if (!strcmp(name, "sockcreate")) {
+		tsec->sockcreate_sid = sid;
+	} else if (!strcmp(name, "current")) {
+		error = -EINVAL;
+		if (sid == 0)
+			goto abort_change;
+
+		/* Only allow single threaded processes to change context */
+		if (!current_is_single_threaded()) {
+			error = security_bounded_transition(&selinux_state,
+							    tsec->sid, sid);
+			if (error)
+				goto abort_change;
+		}
+
+		/* Check permissions for the transition. */
+		error = avc_has_perm(&selinux_state,
+				     tsec->sid, sid, SECCLASS_PROCESS,
+				     PROCESS__DYNTRANSITION, NULL);
+		if (error)
+			goto abort_change;
+
+		/* Check for ptracing, and update the task SID if ok.
+		   Otherwise, leave SID unchanged and fail. */
+		ptsid = ptrace_parent_sid();
+		if (ptsid != 0) {
+			error = avc_has_perm(&selinux_state,
+					     ptsid, sid, SECCLASS_PROCESS,
+					     PROCESS__PTRACE, NULL);
+			if (error)
+				goto abort_change;
+		}
+
+		tsec->sid = sid;
+	} else {
+		error = -EINVAL;
+		goto abort_change;
+	}
+
+	commit_creds(new);
+	return size;
+
+abort_change:
+	abort_creds(new);
+	return error;
+}

+	return selinux_setprocattr(name, value, size);
+}

+static ssize_t susfs_sel_write_context(struct file *file, char *buf, size_t size)
+{
+	struct selinux_fs_info *fsi = file_inode(file)->i_sb->s_fs_info;
+	struct selinux_state *state = fsi->state;
+	char *canon = NULL;
+	u32 sid, len;
+	ssize_t length;
+
+	length = avc_has_perm(&selinux_state,
+			      current_sid(), SECINITSID_SECURITY,
+			      SECCLASS_SECURITY, SECURITY__CHECK_CONTEXT, NULL);
+	if (length)
+		goto out;
+
+	length = susfs_security_context_to_sid(state, buf, size, &sid, GFP_KERNEL);
+	if (length)
+		goto out;
+
+	length = security_sid_to_context(state, sid, &canon, &len);
+	if (length)
+		goto out;
+
+	length = -ERANGE;
+	if (len > SIMPLE_TRANSACTION_LIMIT) {
+		pr_err("SELinux: %s:  context size (%u) exceeds "
+			"payload max\n", __func__, len);
+		goto out;
+	}
+
+	memcpy(buf, canon, len);
+	length = len;
+out:
+	kfree(canon);
+	return length;
+}

+#ifdef CONFIG_KSU_SUSFS
+static ssize_t susfs_sel_write_access(struct file *file, char *buf, size_t size)
+{
+	struct selinux_fs_info *fsi = file_inode(file)->i_sb->s_fs_info;
+	struct selinux_state *state = fsi->state;
+	char *scon = NULL, *tcon = NULL;
+	u32 ssid, tsid;
+	u16 tclass;
+	struct av_decision avd;
+	ssize_t length;
+
+	length = avc_has_perm(&selinux_state,
+			      current_sid(), SECINITSID_SECURITY,
+			      SECCLASS_SECURITY, SECURITY__COMPUTE_AV, NULL);
+	if (length)
+		goto out;
+
+	length = -ENOMEM;
+	scon = kzalloc(size + 1, GFP_KERNEL);
+	if (!scon)
+		goto out;
+
+	length = -ENOMEM;
+	tcon = kzalloc(size + 1, GFP_KERNEL);
+	if (!tcon)
+		goto out;
+
+	length = -EINVAL;
+	if (sscanf(buf, "%s %s %hu", scon, tcon, &tclass) != 3)
+		goto out;
+
+	length = susfs_security_context_str_to_sid(state, scon, &ssid, GFP_KERNEL);
+	if (length)
+		goto out;
+
+	length = susfs_security_context_str_to_sid(state, tcon, &tsid, GFP_KERNEL);
+	if (length)
+		goto out;
+
+	susfs_security_compute_av_user(state, ssid, tsid, tclass, &avd);
+
+	length = scnprintf(buf, SIMPLE_TRANSACTION_LIMIT,
+			  "%x %x %x %x %u %x",
+			  avd.allowed, 0xffffffff,
+			  avd.auditallow, avd.auditdeny,
+			  avd.seqno, avd.flags);
+out:
+	kfree(tcon);
+	kfree(scon);
+	return length;
+}

+#ifdef CONFIG_KSU_SUSFS
+static int susfs_string_to_context_struct(struct policydb *pol,
+				    struct sidtab *sidtabp,
+				    char *scontext,
+				    struct context *ctx,
+				    u32 def_sid)
+{
+	struct role_datum *role;
+	struct type_datum *typdatum;
+	struct user_datum *usrdatum;
+	char *scontextp, *p, oldc;
+	int rc = 0;
+
+	context_init(ctx);
+
+	/* Parse the security context. */
+
+	rc = -EINVAL;
+	scontextp = scontext;
+
+	/* Extract the user. */
+	p = scontextp;
+	while (*p && *p != ':')
+		p++;
+
+	if (*p == 0)
+		goto out;
+
+	*p++ = 0;
+
+	usrdatum = symtab_search(&pol->p_users, scontextp);
+	if (!usrdatum)
+		goto out;
+
+	ctx->user = usrdatum->value;
+
+	/* Extract role. */
+	scontextp = p;
+	while (*p && *p != ':')
+		p++;
+
+	if (*p == 0)
+		goto out;
+
+	*p++ = 0;
+
+	role = symtab_search(&pol->p_roles, scontextp);
+	if (!role)
+		goto out;
+	ctx->role = role->value;
+
+	/* Extract type. */
+	scontextp = p;
+	while (*p && *p != ':')
+		p++;
+	oldc = *p;
+	*p++ = 0;
+
+	typdatum = symtab_search(&pol->p_types, scontextp);
+
+	if (typdatum && !typdatum->attribute) {
+		if (likely(susfs_is_sus_type_rcu(typdatum->value)))
+			goto out;
+		goto orig_flow;
+	}
+
+	goto out;
+
+orig_flow:
+	ctx->type = typdatum->value;
+
+	rc = mls_context_to_sid(pol, oldc, p, ctx, sidtabp, def_sid);
+	if (rc)
+		goto out;
+
+	/* Check the validity of the new context. */
+	rc = -EINVAL;
+	if (!policydb_context_isvalid(pol, ctx))
+		goto out;
+	rc = 0;
+out:
+	if (rc)
+		context_destroy(ctx);
+	return rc;
+}
+
+static int susfs_security_context_to_sid_core(struct selinux_state *state,
+					const char *scontext, u32 scontext_len,
+					u32 *sid, u32 def_sid, gfp_t gfp_flags,
+					int force)
+{
+	struct selinux_policy *policy;
+	struct policydb *policydb;
+	struct sidtab *sidtab;
+	char *scontext2, *str = NULL;
+	struct context context;
+	int rc = 0;
+
+	/* An empty security context is never valid. */
+	if (!scontext_len)
+		return -EINVAL;
+
+	/* Copy the string to allow changes and ensure a NUL terminator */
+	scontext2 = kmemdup_nul(scontext, scontext_len, gfp_flags);
+	if (!scontext2)
+		return -ENOMEM;
+
+	if (!selinux_initialized(state)) {
+		int i;
+
+		for (i = 1; i < SECINITSID_NUM; i++) {
+			const char *s = initial_sid_to_string[i];
+
+			if (s && !strcmp(s, scontext2)) {
+				*sid = i;
+				goto out;
+			}
+		}
+		*sid = SECINITSID_KERNEL;
+		goto out;
+	}
+	*sid = SECSID_NULL;
+
+	if (force) {
+		/* Save another copy for storing in uninterpreted form */
+		rc = -ENOMEM;
+		str = kstrdup(scontext2, gfp_flags);
+		if (!str)
+			goto out;
+	}
+retry:
+	rcu_read_lock();
+	policy = rcu_dereference(state->policy);
+	policydb = &policy->policydb;
+	sidtab = policy->sidtab;
+	rc = susfs_string_to_context_struct(policydb, sidtab, scontext2,
+				      &context, def_sid);
+	if (rc == -EINVAL && force) {
+		context.str = str;
+		context.len = strlen(str) + 1;
+		str = NULL;
+	} else if (rc)
+		goto out_unlock;
+	rc = sidtab_context_to_sid(sidtab, &context, sid);
+	if (rc == -ESTALE) {
+		rcu_read_unlock();
+		if (context.str) {
+			str = context.str;
+			context.str = NULL;
+		}
+		context_destroy(&context);
+		goto retry;
+	}
+	context_destroy(&context);
+out_unlock:
+	rcu_read_unlock();
+out:
+	kfree(scontext2);
+	kfree(str);
+	return rc;
+}
+
+int susfs_security_context_to_sid(struct selinux_state *state,
+			    const char *scontext, u32 scontext_len, u32 *sid,
+			    gfp_t gfp)
+{
+	return susfs_security_context_to_sid_core(state, scontext, scontext_len,
+					    sid, SECSID_NULL, gfp, 0);
+}
+
+int susfs_security_context_str_to_sid(struct selinux_state *state,
+				const char *scontext, u32 *sid, gfp_t gfp)
+{
+	return susfs_security_context_to_sid(state, scontext, strlen(scontext),
+				       sid, gfp);
+}
+
+void susfs_security_compute_av_user(struct selinux_state *state,
+			      u32 ssid,
+			      u32 tsid,
+			      u16 tclass,
+			      struct av_decision *avd)
+{
+	struct selinux_policy *policy;
+	struct policydb *policydb;
+	struct sidtab *sidtab;
+	struct context *scontext = NULL, *tcontext = NULL;
+
+	rcu_read_lock();
+	policy = rcu_dereference(state->policy);
+	avd_init(policy, avd);
+	if (!selinux_initialized(state))
+		goto allow;
+
+	policydb = &policy->policydb;
+	sidtab = policy->sidtab;
+
+	scontext = sidtab_search(sidtab, ssid);
+	if (!scontext) {
+		pr_err("SELinux: %s:  unrecognized SID %d\n",
+		       __func__, ssid);
+		goto out;
+	}
+
+	/* permissive domain? */
+	if (ebitmap_get_bit(&policydb->permissive_map, scontext->type))
+		avd->flags |= AVD_FLAGS_PERMISSIVE;
+
+	tcontext = sidtab_search(sidtab, tsid);
+	if (!tcontext) {
+		pr_err("SELinux: %s:  unrecognized SID %d\n",
+		       __func__, tsid);
+		goto out;
+	}
+
+	if (unlikely(!tclass)) {
+		if (policydb->allow_unknown)
+			goto allow;
+		goto out;
+	}
+
+	context_struct_compute_av(policydb, scontext, tcontext, tclass, avd,
+				  NULL);
+	susfs_spoof_avd_allowed_rcu(scontext->type, tcontext->type, tclass, &avd->allowed);
+ out:
+	rcu_read_unlock();
+	return;
+allow:
+	avd->allowed = 0xffffffff;
+	goto out;
+}
+#endif // #ifdef CONFIG_KSU_SUSFS

 /*
  * The selinux_status_page shall be exposed to userspace applications
  * using mmap interface on /selinux/status.
@@ -85,6 +89,15 @@ void selinux_status_update_setenforce(struct selinux_state *state,
 	if (state->status_page) {
 		status = page_address(state->status_page);
 
+#ifdef CONFIG_KSU_SUSFS

+			smp_wmb();

+			smp_wmb();

@@ -111,6 +124,17 @@ void selinux_status_update_policyload(struct selinux_state *state,

`release-notes/kernelsu-next.md`:

```md
Status of this build on the Meizu mBlu 21: `boot-<variant>.img` has not been tried on a phone, and the kernel path does not boot there (a SukiSU-Ultra kernel image written to `boot_a` on 2026-11-03 stopped at the logo). Use the `init_boot` LKM path; the images tested on the phone are in release `mblu21-root-20261102`.

This pre-release carries two flashable images for the `kernelsu-next` root path:

| File | Partition | Contents |
|---|---|---|
| `boot-kernelsu-next.img` | `boot` | GKI `5.15.149` kernel built from `android13-5.15-2024-07_r1` with the `kernelsu-next` driver integrated; AVB footer re-signed with the public AOSP test key |
| `init_boot-kernelsu-next-lkm.img` | `init_boot` | stock init_boot ramdisk with the `kernelsu-next` LKM injection (no kernel change) |

`susfs_guard_lkm.ko` is the official KernelSU-Next SUSFS module for `android13-5.15` (release `v3.4.2`); the manager loads it, the kernel needs no SUSFS source patch.

Pick one path, not both. Both require an unlocked bootloader.

Static validation attached to this release:

- `abi-vendor_boot.txt` - every symbol the 139 stock first-stage modules in `vendor_boot` import, compared against the built kernel's `Module.symvers`
- `abi-vendor_dlkm.txt` - every symbol the 113 stock `vendor_dlkm` modules import, compared against the built kernel's `Module.symvers`
- `abi-system_dlkm.txt` - the same check for the stock `system_dlkm` modules
- `config-report.txt` - kernel configuration of the built image
- `lkm-kernelsu-next.log` - how the init_boot image was patched

Every file has a matching `.sha256`.

`release-notes/mblu21-pixelos-root-20261105.md`:

```md
Root for the unofficial **PixelOS 17** ROM on the Meizu mBlu 21 ([tickernelz/pixelos_build_mblu21](https://github.com/tickernelz/pixelos_build_mblu21), build `PixelOS_mblu21-17.0-20261105-1507`): SukiSU-Ultra `v4.3.0-r2` built from source and loaded as an LKM from `init_boot`. **Tested on hardware** on 2026-11-05 (results below). The stock-ROM images in `mblu21-root-20261102` are unchanged and remain the ones for the stock Meizu firmware.

## Source

| Component | Source | Pin |
|---|---|---|
| `kernelsu.ko`, `ksud`, `ksuinit` | [SukiSU-Ultra/SukiSU-Ultra](https://github.com/SukiSU-Ultra/SukiSU-Ultra/tree/v4.3.0-r2) | tag `v4.3.0-r2`, commit `645ef9860b9b7f900bb5d1b27e224f2c311d39c0`, version code 41150 |
| SUSFS guard | KernelSU-Next SUSFS guard, unchanged from `mblu21-susfs-lkm-20261102` | `susfs_guard_lkm.ko` `024aa0de…`, SUSFS `v2.3.0`, GKI |
| `init_boot` input | `IMAGES/init_boot.img` of PixelOS build 1507 (built from source: generic ramdisk only) | `738c81512a8d1bd2…` |
| Manager | upstream release asset, not re-signed | `SukiSU_v4.3.0-r2_41150-release.apk` `6fe64e7b…` |

Between `v4.3.0` (used before) and `v4.3.0-r2` only 17 files under `kernel/` changed; `userspace/ksud` and `uapi/` are identical, so the SUSFS ABI `ksud` speaks is the same. `v4.3.0-r1` to `v4.3.0-r2` changed only `manager/`. The 20 `ksu_*` symbols the guard resolves by name exist in both `v4.3.0` and `v4.3.0-r2` (the one `ksu_*` symbol removed in r2, `ksu_show_allow_list`, is not used by the guard), so the guard was not rebuilt. The newest upstream `tiann/KernelSU` kernel commit (`28db74f6`, #3964) is already in r2 as `85d05cf2`.

## Build

`build-sukisu.sh` builds `kernelsu.ko` in the same container the upstream CI uses (`ghcr.io/ylarod/ddk-min:android13-5.15-20260828`, `CONFIG_KSU=m CONFIG_KPM=y`, network off), then `ksuinit` and `ksud` with NDK r29 using the upstream workflow flags, with that `kernelsu.ko` and `ksuinit` embedded in `ksud`.

- `lkm-aarch64-android13-5.15_kernelsu-v4.3.0-r2.ko` is **byte-identical** to the upstream release asset `lkm-aarch64-android13-5.15_kernelsu.ko` (`f0d3f0a3…`).
- `ksud` and `ksuinit` differ in bytes from the upstream assets (`dec34535…`, `c7034b99…`); same source, different host toolchain. `ksud` bytes also varied between two local runs of the same source; `kernelsu.ko` and `ksuinit` did not.
- `build-sukisu-v4.3.0-r2.log` is the log of the run that produced these binaries. That run also tried a host (x86_64) `ksud` build, which does not compile at r2 (`error: could not compile ksud ... 18 previous errors`) after the Android binary was already built; that step was removed from the attached script.

`patch-init-boot.sh <stock init_boot.img> <out.img>` runs the Android `ksud boot-patch` on the host under `qemu-aarch64-static` with bionic from the PixelOS build output. Result ramdisk: `init` = `ksuinit`, `kernelsu.ko` = `f0d3f0a3…`, `init.real` = the original `init`. A patched `init_boot` matches one ROM build only: repatch from each new build's `init_boot.img`.

## Install (from an already rooted PixelOS 1507)

```sh
sha256sum -c SHA256SUMS
adb install -r SukiSU_v4.3.0-r2_41150-release.apk
adb push init_boot-PixelOS_mblu21-17.0-20261105-1507-sukisu-v4.3.0-r2.img /data/local/tmp/ib.img
adb shell su -c 'dd if=/dev/block/by-name/init_boot$(getprop ro.boot.slot_suffix) of=/data/local/tmp/init_boot-backup.img'
adb shell su -c 'dd if=/data/local/tmp/ib.img of=/dev/block/by-name/init_boot$(getprop ro.boot.slot_suffix) conv=fsync'
adb reboot
```

Pull `/data/local/tmp/init_boot-backup.img` to the PC before rebooting; writing it back the same way undoes the change. From an unrooted phone, write the image with `spd_dump` as in [docs/ROOT.md](https://github.com/tickernelz/android_kernel_meizu_mblu21/blob/main/docs/ROOT.md) (partition `init_boot_a` or `init_boot_b`, the active slot). The SUSFS guard module installs as in `mblu21-susfs-lkm-20261102`.

## Tested on hardware (2026-11-05, slot `_b`, `ro.build.date.utc=1793890890`)

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| `ksud -V` | `ksud 4.3.0-r2 (uapi: 6)` |
| `/proc/modules` | `kernelsu 561152 1 - Live 0x0000000000000000 (OE)` |

| `su -c id`, `/system/bin/su -c id` | `uid=0(root) … context=u:r:ksu:s0` |
| `getenforce` | `Enforcing` |
| Data | kept, `user_setup_complete=1` (no wipe) |
| Stability | 0 panic/Oops in `dmesg`, `logcat -b crash` empty |
| Kernel | stock Meizu boot image (`uname` `5.15.149-android13-8-00008-gbe074b05e5af-ab12096863`), no KernelSU built in |

Raw output: `verify-on-phone-20261105.txt`.

Not tested: the `spd_dump` write path with this image, a phone not already rooted, and the stock Meizu ROM with r2. Observed once and not explained: after the reboot into this image, USB came up as MTP only and adb stayed off for 14 minutes (`AdbService` read `adb_enabled=0` at boot) until USB debugging was switched on again in Developer options.

The kernel path (`boot-<variant>.img`) still does not boot on this phone; see the README.

`root/integrity-module/README.md`:

```md
# mblu21 integrity module

One KernelSU/Magisk zip for Play Integrity on the Meizu mBlu 21 (M416H): Tricky Store
1.4.1 handles keystore attestation, Play Integrity Fork v19 supplies the certified
fingerprint and prop cleanup, and this module owns the target list, the security patch
level and the keybox policy. It replaces the combined third-party drop-in that occupied
`/data/adb/tricky_store` and fetched a keybox over plain HTTP at boot.

| File | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `module/module.prop` | module identity: id `playintegrityfix`, version `v1.0.0` |
| `module/customize.sh` | installer: moves the old `/data/adb/modules/tricky_store` aside, keeps the keybox already on the phone, seeds the config |
| `module/post-fs-data.sh`, `module/service.sh` | source the unmodified upstream PIF scripts, then start the Tricky Store daemon from the module root |
| `module/bin/integrity.sh` | `status`, `doctor`, `verdict`, `keybox`, `targets`, `sp` |
| `module/bin/keybox.sh` | keybox completeness report and the opt-in fetch |
| `module/bin/boot-verdict.sh`, `module/bin/verdict-parse.sh` | measure the verdict 60 s after every boot and append one parsed line to `/data/adb/tricky_store/verdicts.jsonl`; it taps only when the checker has focus and records `skipped:<focus>` otherwise |
| `module/bin/fix-persist.sh` | restore `/data/property/persistent_properties` to 0600 and re-arm `persist.sys.usb.config=adb` |
| `module/bin/targets.sh`, `module/bin/sp.sh` | target list and security patch level helpers |
| `module/target.txt`, `module/security_patch.txt`, `module/pif.prop`, `module/integrity.conf` | the defaults the installer seeds |
| `module/licenses/` | upstream notices and the GPL-3.0 text |
| `build-module.sh` | downloads the pinned upstream releases and assembles the zip |
| `tests/run.sh` | shell tests for the config logic, no device needed |
| `tools/measure-spic.sh` | asks the phone for a fresh Play Integrity verdict over adb |

The module id is `playintegrityfix` on purpose: the upstream PIF companion library opens
`/data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/classes.dex` and `/data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/pif.prop`
by absolute path, so the module has to live there.

The Tricky Store payload keeps its upstream layout at the module root — `service.apk`,
`daemon`, `libtricky_store.so`, `machikado`, `mazoku` — because `daemon` starts
`app_process` with `-Djava.class.path=./service.apk`, so its working directory has to be the
directory that holds the APK and the shared library. Only `post-fs-data.sh`, `service.sh`
and `target.txt` collide with the PIF payload, and those are merged here.

## Build

```sh
./build-module.sh mblu21-integrity.zip
```

Both upstream artifacts are pinned by sha256 in the script and pulled from their official
releases; they are not vendored in this repository. Rebuilding is reproducible: entries
carry a fixed timestamp, so the same inputs give the same sha256.

## Install

```sh
adb push mblu21-integrity.zip /data/local/tmp/
adb shell ksud module install /data/local/tmp/mblu21-integrity.zip
adb reboot
adb shell sh /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/bin/integrity.sh doctor
```

`customize.sh` keeps an existing `/data/adb/tricky_store/keybox.xml` untouched and moves
every other artifact of the old module into `/data/adb/tricky_store/legacy-<stamp>/`.

## Keybox policy

`/data/adb/tricky_store/keybox.xml` is chosen by `keybox_mode` in
`/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf`:

| `keybox_mode` | Behaviour |
|---|---|
| `auto` (default) | keep the keybox already on the phone; install the bundled one only when none exists |
| `existing` | never install the bundled one, whatever is there stays |
| `software` | install the bundled one, but only when the content actually differs |
| `software` | install the software keybox that ships inside the Tricky Store release, backing up the previous file next to it |

The bundled software keybox is `software-keybox.xml` in the module and inside the official
Tricky Store zip (`DeviceID="sw"`); it carries both an `ecdsa` and an `rsa` key, which is
what Play asks for. It is a self-signed test chain, not a Google-signed one.

The module never downloads a keybox by default: `auto_keybox=0` and `keybox_url` is
empty. Turning it on is an explicit decision, and `service.sh` warns every time it runs:

* an `http://` source is plain HTTP, so anything on the path can substitute the file;
* `keybox_sha256` is a change detector, not a signature, so leaving it empty trusts
  whatever arrives;
* a keybox carries another device's private attestation keys, which is why no keybox is
  vendored here and why the shipped config does not name a source.

`bin/integrity.sh keybox` reports whether the file on the phone is complete: Play asks
for both an `ecdsa` and an `rsa` key, and an `ecdsa`-only file makes Play log
`Incomplete keybox provided by overlays` and fall back to the real TEE record. That log
line, not a guess, is what `doctor` fails on.

## Security patch level

`pif.prop` spoofs `ro.build.*` inside Play Services and Play Store, so the identity Google
evaluates is the fingerprint in that file, not the ROM's own `ro.build.id`. The security
patch level therefore has to match the spoofed fingerprint's build id, and
`bin/sp.sh check` verifies exactly that: the three lines in `security_patch.txt`, the
`SECURITY_PATCH` in `pif.prop` and the `YYMMDD` stamp inside the fingerprint's build id
must agree, and the level must sit on the 5th.

```sh
adb shell sh /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/bin/integrity.sh sp show
adb shell sh /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/bin/integrity.sh sp set 2026-11-05
```

## Measure

Every boot measures itself: `service.sh` starts `bin/boot-verdict.sh`, which waits for
`sys.boot_completed`, waits another 60 s, drives Simple Play Integrity Checker and appends the
parsed result to `/data/adb/tricky_store/verdicts.jsonl`. `auto_verdict=0` in
`/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf` turns that off.

```sh
adb shell sh /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/bin/integrity.sh verdicts 5
adb shell sh /data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/bin/integrity.sh doctor
```

`doctor` also checks the hiding invariants: SUSFS active, no `/system/bin/su` in the default
namespace, `persistent_properties` at 0600, and `persist.sys.usb.config` still carrying `adb`.

## Measure on demand

```sh
./tools/measure-spic.sh
```

It wakes the phone, resets the Simple Play Integrity Checker, taps its request button and
prints the raw `System.out` JSON from logcat. The tap coordinates live in
`integrity.conf` (`spic_tap_x`, `spic_tap_y`).

## What this module cannot do

`MEETS_STRONG_INTEGRITY` on Android 13+ needs hardware-backed proof that the bootloader is
locked and the OS is a certified build. This phone's bootloader is unlocked. The only way
to make such a claim verify is an attestation chain signed by an unrevoked Google keybox,
which is another device's secret material. The module therefore targets
`MEETS_DEVICE_INTEGRITY` and reports whatever it measures.

```

`root/integrity-module/build-module.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
here=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
root=$(cd "$here/../.." && pwd)

ts_url=https://github.com/5ec1cff/TrickyStore/releases/download/1.4.1/Tricky-Store-v1.4.1-245-72b2e84-release.zip
ts_sha=2f5e73fcba0e4e43b6e96b38f333cbe394873e3a81cf8fe1b831c2fbd6c46ea9
pif_url=https://github.com/osm0sis/PlayIntegrityFork/releases/download/v19/PlayIntegrityFork-v19.zip
pif_sha=83b4d5f32743805c5b5a69361d73b8ab9c095325e24f20711afa1abea837b58f
stamp=1767225600

out=${1:-$root/mblu21-integrity.zip}
cache=${2:-${XDG_CACHE_HOME:-$HOME/.cache}/mblu21-integrity}

fetch() {
    url=$1
    sha=$2
    dest=$3
    if [ -f "$dest" ] && printf '%s  %s\n' "$sha" "$dest" | sha256sum -c - >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        return 0
    fi
    mkdir -p "$(dirname "$dest")"
    curl -fsSL -o "$dest.part" "$url"
    printf '%s  %s\n' "$sha" "$dest.part" | sha256sum -c - >/dev/null
    mv "$dest.part" "$dest"
}

work=$(mktemp -d)
trap 'rm -rf "$work"' EXIT
fetch "$ts_url" "$ts_sha" "$cache/Tricky-Store-1.4.1.zip"
fetch "$pif_url" "$pif_sha" "$cache/PlayIntegrityFork-v19.zip"

mkdir -p "$work/ts" "$work/pif" "$work/stage/zygisk"
unzip -q "$cache/Tricky-Store-1.4.1.zip" -d "$work/ts"
unzip -q "$cache/PlayIntegrityFork-v19.zip" -d "$work/pif"
cp -a "$here/module/." "$work/stage/"

for f in service.apk daemon mazoku machikado.arm64 sepolicy.rule; do
    cp "$work/ts/$f" "$work/stage/$f"
done
cp "$work/ts/machikado.arm64" "$work/stage/machikado"
cp "$work/ts/lib/arm64/libtricky_store.so" "$work/stage/libtricky_store.so"
cp "$work/ts/keybox.xml" "$work/stage/software-keybox.xml"

for f in service.apk daemon mazoku machikado.arm64 lib/arm64/libtricky_store.so keybox.xml; do
    want=$(cat "$work/ts/$f.sha256")
    got=$(sha256sum "$work/ts/$f" | cut -d' ' -f1)
    [ "$want" = "$got" ] || { echo "upstream sha256 mismatch for $f" >&2; exit 1; }
done

for f in classes.dex common_func.sh common_setup.sh app_replace_list.txt example.pif.prop; do
    cp "$work/pif/$f" "$work/stage/"
done
cp "$work/pif/post-fs-data.sh" "$work/stage/pif-post-fs-data.sh"
cp "$work/pif/service.sh" "$work/stage/pif-service.sh"
cp "$work/pif/zygisk/"*.so "$work/stage/zygisk/"
mkdir -p "$work/stage/META-INF/com/google/android"
cp "$work/pif/META-INF/com/google/android/update-binary" "$work/stage/META-INF/com/google/android/update-binary"
cp "$work/pif/META-INF/com/google/android/updater-script" "$work/stage/META-INF/com/google/android/updater-script"

chmod 755 "$work/stage"/*.sh "$work/stage"/bin/*.sh "$work/stage"/daemon "$work/stage"/machikado "$work/stage"/mazoku
chmod 644 "$work/stage/service.apk" "$work/stage/libtricky_store.so" "$work/stage/classes.dex" "$work/stage/pif.prop" "$work/stage/target.txt" "$work/stage/security_patch.txt" "$work/stage/integrity.conf" "$work/stage/software-keybox.xml"
find "$work/stage" -exec touch -m -d "@$stamp" {} +

rm -f "$out"
( cd "$work/stage" && zip -q -X -r "$out" . )
printf 'built %s\n' "$out"
sha256sum "$out"

```

`root/integrity-module/module/action.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
MODPATH="${0%/*}"
exec sh "$MODPATH/bin/integrity.sh" status

`root/integrity-module/module/bin/boot-verdict.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
ROOT=${INTEGRITY_ROOT:-}
TS=$ROOT/data/adb/tricky_store
BIN=$(dirname -- "$0")
OUT=$TS/verdicts.jsonl
PKG=com.henrikherzig.playintegritychecker

. "$BIN/lib.sh"

[ "$(conf_get auto_verdict)" = 0 ] && exit 0

for _ in $(seq 1 120); do
    [ "$(getprop sys.boot_completed 2>/dev/null)" = 1 ] && break
    sleep 5
done

delay=$(conf_get verdict_delay)
[ -n "$delay" ] || delay=60
sleep "$delay"

key=$(conf_get spic_unlock_key)
[ -n "$key" ] && input keyevent "$key" 2>/dev/null

x=$(conf_get spic_tap_x)
y=$(conf_get spic_tap_y)
[ -n "$x" ] || x=360
[ -n "$y" ] || y=594

am force-stop "$PKG" 2>/dev/null
am start -W -n "$PKG/.MainActivity" >/dev/null 2>&1

focus=""
for _ in $(seq 1 8); do
    focus=$(dumpsys window 2>/dev/null | sed -n 's/.*mCurrentFocus=Window{[^ ]* [^ ]* \([^}]*\)}.*/\1/p' | head -n 1)
    case $focus in
        *"$PKG"*) break ;;
    esac
    sleep 1
done

if [ -z "$focus" ]; then
    printf '{"ts":"%s","boot_id":"%s","device":"skipped:no-focus","app":"none","licensing":"none"}\n' \
        "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')" "$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)" >> "$OUT"
    exit 0
fi

case $focus in
    *"$PKG"*) : ;;
    *)
        printf '{"ts":"%s","boot_id":"%s","device":"skipped:%s","app":"none","licensing":"none"}\n' \
            "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')" "$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)" "$focus" >> "$OUT"
        exit 0
        ;;
esac

mark=$(date '+%m-%d %H:%M:%S.000')
input tap "$x" "$y" 2>/dev/null
sleep 9

raw=$(logcat -d -v brief -T "$mark" 2>/dev/null | grep -F 'System.out' | sed 's/^.*System.out( *[0-9]*): //')
mkdir -p "$TS" 2>/dev/null
printf '%s' "$raw" | sh "$BIN/verdict-parse.sh" >> "$OUT"

```

`root/integrity-module/module/bin/fix-persist.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
ROOT=${INTEGRITY_ROOT:-}
PF=$ROOT/data/property/persistent_properties
LOGDIR=$ROOT/data/adb/tricky_store
BIN=$(dirname -- "$0")

if [ -f "$BIN/lib.sh" ]; then
    . "$BIN/lib.sh"
fi

log() {
    mkdir -p "$LOGDIR" 2>/dev/null
    printf '[%s] %s\n' "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')" "$*" >> "$LOGDIR/integrity.log" 2>/dev/null
    printf '%s\n' "$*"
}

if [ -f "$PF" ]; then
    mode=$(stat -c '%a' "$PF" 2>/dev/null)
    if [ "$mode" != 600 ]; then
        chown root:root "$PF" 2>/dev/null
        chmod 600 "$PF" 2>/dev/null
        log "repaired $PF mode $mode -> 600 so init accepts it on the next boot"
    fi
fi

if command -v getprop >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    keep=$(conf_get keep_adb)
    [ -n "$keep" ] || keep=1
    if [ "$keep" = 1 ]; then
        case "$(getprop persist.sys.usb.config)" in
            *adb*) : ;;
            *)
                setprop persist.sys.usb.config adb 2>/dev/null
                log "re-armed persist.sys.usb.config=adb (keep_adb=1)"
                ;;
        esac
    fi
fi

```

`root/integrity-module/module/bin/integrity.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
bin_dir=$(dirname -- "$0")
: "${INTEGRITY_BIN:=$bin_dir}"
. "$INTEGRITY_BIN/lib.sh"
. "$INTEGRITY_BIN/keybox.sh"

PKG=com.henrikherzig.playintegritychecker

status() {
    printf 'module: %s %s\n' "$(file_get "$MOD_DIR/module.prop" id)" "$(file_get "$MOD_DIR/module.prop" version)"
    printf 'tricky store payload: %s\n' "$([ -f "$MOD_DIR/service.apk" ] && echo present || echo missing)"
    printf 'pif companion: %s\n' "$([ -f "$MOD_DIR/zygisk/arm64-v8a.so" ] && echo present || echo missing)"
    printf 'targets: %s entries\n' "$(grep -cv '^#' "$TARGETS_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' ')"
    printf 'security patch: %s\n' "$(file_get "$PIF_PROP" SECURITY_PATCH)"
    printf 'keybox_mode: %s, ts_daemon: %s, auto_verdict: %s, keep_adb: %s\n' \
        "$(conf_get keybox_mode)" "$(conf_get ts_daemon)" "$(conf_get auto_verdict)" "$(conf_get keep_adb)"
    keybox_report
    printf 'last verdict in logcat: %s\n' "$(logcat -d -v brief 2>/dev/null | grep -o 'MEETS_[A-Z_]*' | tail -n 1)"
}

launch_count() {
    logcat -d -v brief 2>/dev/null | grep -c 'tricky_store.MainKt'
}

doctor() {
    rc=0
    printf '== module\n'
    if [ -f "$MOD_DIR/module.prop" ]; then
        printf '[ok] %s %s\n' "$(file_get "$MOD_DIR/module.prop" id)" "$(file_get "$MOD_DIR/module.prop" version)"
    else
        printf '[fail] %s is missing\n' "$MOD_DIR/module.prop"
        rc=1
    fi
    if [ -e "$MOD_DIR/disable" ]; then
        printf '[fail] the module is disabled\n'
        rc=1
    fi
    for f in classes.dex zygisk/arm64-v8a.so service.apk libtricky_store.so machikado daemon pif.prop; do
        if [ ! -e "$MOD_DIR/$f" ]; then
            printf '[fail] missing %s\n' "$f"
            rc=1
        fi
    done

    printf '== keystore daemon\n'
    ts_wanted=$(conf_get ts_daemon)
    if [ "$ts_wanted" != 1 ]; then
        printf '[ok] the TrickyStore loop is off (ts_daemon=%s); the daemon cannot help while it does not inject\n' "${ts_wanted:-0}"
    else
        first=$(launch_count)
        sleep 10
        second=$(launch_count)
        rate=$((second - first))
        if [ "$rate" -gt 3 ]; then
            printf '[fail] the daemon respawned %s times in 10 s, which burns CPU without injecting\n' "$rate"
            rc=1
        elif pgrep -f "$MOD_DIR/service.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
            printf '[ok] the loop is alive and respawned %s times in 10 s\n' "$rate"
        else
            printf '[fail] ts_daemon=1 but the loop is not running\n'
            rc=1
        fi
    fi

    printf '== zygisk\n'
    if [ -d "$INTEGRITY_ROOT/data/adb/modules/zygisksu" ] || [ -d "$INTEGRITY_ROOT/data/adb/modules/zygisk_next" ]; then
        printf '[ok] a zygisk provider module is installed\n'
    else
        printf '[fail] no zygisk provider: the PIF companion cannot load\n'
        rc=1
    fi

    printf '== keybox\n'
    keybox_report
    algs=$(keybox_algs)
    state=${algs%% *}
    rest=${algs#* }
    rsa=${rest##* }
    if [ "$state" = present ] && [ "$rsa" -eq 0 ]; then
        printf '[warn] the keybox has no rsa key, so Play falls back to the real TEE record; that is the
'
        printf '       expected state when the owner keybox is kept and BASIC is the goal
'
    fi

    printf '== security patch\n'
    if sp_out=$(sh "$INTEGRITY_BIN/sp.sh" check 2>&1); then
        printf '[ok] %s\n' "$(file_get "$PIF_PROP" SECURITY_PATCH)"
    else
        printf '%s\n' "$sp_out"
        rc=1
    fi

    printf '== target list\n'
    if tg_out=$(sh "$INTEGRITY_BIN/targets.sh" check 2>&1); then
        printf '[ok] Play Services, Play Store and the checker are in scope\n'
    else
        printf '%s\n' "$tg_out"
        rc=1
    fi

    printf '== rom prop hooks\n'
    if [ -n "$(getprop net.pixelos.version 2>/dev/null)" ]; then
        if [ "$(getprop persist.sys.pihooks.disable.gms_props 2>/dev/null)" = true ]; then
            printf '[ok] PixelOS prop imitation hooks are disabled for gms\n'
        else
            printf '[fail] PixelOS prop imitation hooks are active and fight the PIF props\n'
            rc=1
        fi
    else
        printf '[ok] no custom rom prop hook detected\n'
    fi

    printf '== hiding\n'
    if command -v ksud >/dev/null 2>&1 && [ "$(ksud susfs status 2>/dev/null)" = true ]; then
        printf '[ok] SUSFS is active\n'
    else
        printf '[fail] SUSFS is not active\n'
        rc=1
    fi
    if [ -e /system/bin/su ]; then
        printf '[fail] /system/bin/su is visible in the default namespace\n'
        rc=1
    else
        printf '[ok] no /system/bin/su in the default namespace\n'
    fi
    pf=/data/property/persistent_properties
    pmode=$(stat -c '%a' "$pf" 2>/dev/null)
    if [ "$pmode" = 600 ]; then
        printf '[ok] persistent properties are 0600\n'
    else
        printf '[fail] %s is %s, init will drop every persistent property\n' "$pf" "$pmode"
        rc=1
    fi
    case "$(getprop persist.sys.usb.config 2>/dev/null)" in
        *adb*) printf '[ok] persist.sys.usb.config carries adb\n' ;;
        *) printf '[fail] persist.sys.usb.config lost adb\n'; rc=1 ;;
    esac

    printf '== verdict\n'
    last=$(logcat -d -v brief 2>/dev/null | grep -o 'MEETS_[A-Z_]*' | tail -n 1)
    printf '[info] last device verdict in logcat: %s\n' "${last:-none}"
    if [ -f "$TS_DIR/verdicts.jsonl" ]; then
        printf '[info] last recorded boot: %s\n' "$(tail -n 1 "$TS_DIR/verdicts.jsonl")"
    fi
    return $rc
}

shown_focus() {
    dumpsys window 2>/dev/null | sed -n 's/.*mCurrentFocus=Window{[^ ]* [^ ]* \([^}]*\)}.*/\1/p' | head -n 1
}

verdict() {
    x=$(conf_get spic_tap_x)
    y=$(conf_get spic_tap_y)
    [ -n "$x" ] || x=360
    [ -n "$y" ] || y=594
    input keyevent KEYCODE_WAKEUP 2>/dev/null
    wm dismiss-keyguard 2>/dev/null
    sleep 1
    am force-stop "$PKG" 2>/dev/null
    am start -W -n "$PKG/.MainActivity" >/dev/null 2>&1
    focus=""
    for _ in $(seq 1 8); do
        focus=$(shown_focus)
        case $focus in
            *"$PKG"*) break ;;
        esac
        sleep 1
    done
    case $focus in
        *"$PKG"*) : ;;
        *)
            printf 'verdict skipped: the checker does not have focus (current: %s)\n' "${focus:-unknown}" >&2
            return 3
            ;;
    esac
    mark=$(date '+%m-%d %H:%M:%S.000')
    input tap "$x" "$y" 2>/dev/null
    sleep 9
    logcat -d -v brief -T "$mark" 2>/dev/null | grep -F 'System.out' | sed 's/^.*System.out( *[0-9]*): //'
}

case ${1:-status} in
    status) status ;;
    doctor) doctor ;;
    verdict) verdict ;;
    keybox) keybox_report ;;
    keybox-mode)
        shift
        if [ -n "${1:-}" ]; then
            file_set "$CONF" keybox_mode "$1" && keybox_apply_mode
        else
            printf 'keybox_mode: %s\n' "$(conf_get keybox_mode)"
        fi
        ;;
    fix-adb) sh "$INTEGRITY_BIN/fix-persist.sh" && printf 'persistent properties checked\n' ;;
    verdicts)
        shift
        n=${1:-3}
        if [ -f "$TS_DIR/verdicts.jsonl" ]; then
            tail -n "$n" "$TS_DIR/verdicts.jsonl"
        else
            printf 'no verdicts recorded yet\n'
        fi
        ;;
    targets) shift; sh "$INTEGRITY_BIN/targets.sh" "$@" ;;
    sp) shift; sh "$INTEGRITY_BIN/sp.sh" "$@" ;;
    *)
        printf 'usage: integrity.sh status|doctor|verdict|keybox|keybox-mode <mode>|fix-adb|verdicts [n]|targets <cmd>|sp <cmd>\n' >&2
        exit 2
        ;;
esac

```

`root/integrity-module/module/bin/keybox.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
bin_dir=$(dirname -- "$0")
: "${INTEGRITY_BIN:=$bin_dir}"
. "$INTEGRITY_BIN/lib.sh"

keybox_algs() {
    if [ ! -f "$KEYBOX" ]; then
        printf 'none 0 0\n'
        return 0
    fi
    ec=$(grep -c 'algorithm="ecdsa"' "$KEYBOX" 2>/dev/null)
    rsa=$(grep -c 'algorithm="rsa"' "$KEYBOX" 2>/dev/null)
    printf 'present %s %s\n' "${ec:-0}" "${rsa:-0}"
}

keybox_report() {
    algs=$(keybox_algs)
    state=${algs%% *}
    rest=${algs#* }
    ec=${rest%% *}
    rsa=${rest##* }
    if [ "$state" = none ]; then
        printf 'keybox: none\n'
        printf '  TrickyStore runs certificate-hack mode only: it rewrites the attestation the real TEE returns.\n'
        return 0
    fi
    if grep -q 'DeviceID="sw"' "$KEYBOX" 2>/dev/null; then
        printf 'keybox: %s bytes, ecdsa=%s rsa=%s, bundled software keybox (self-signed test chain)\n' "$(wc -c < "$KEYBOX" | tr -d ' ')" "$ec" "$rsa"
    else
        printf 'keybox: %s bytes, ecdsa=%s rsa=%s\n' "$(wc -c < "$KEYBOX" | tr -d ' ')" "$ec" "$rsa"
    fi
    if [ "$ec" -eq 0 ]; then
        printf '  WARN: no ecdsa key, and Play asks for an EC attestation record.\n'
    fi
    if [ "$rsa" -eq 0 ]; then
        printf '  WARN: no rsa key. Play logs "Incomplete keybox provided by overlays" and falls back to the\n'
        printf '        real TEE record, so the verdict stays at MEETS_BASIC_INTEGRITY.\n'
        printf '        The rsa private key cannot be derived from the ecdsa one; only a complete keybox fixes it.\n'
    fi
    if [ "$ec" -gt 0 ] && [ "$rsa" -gt 0 ]; then
        printf '  complete: both ecdsa and rsa keys are present\n'
    fi
    return 0
}

keybox_auto_apply() {
    [ "$(conf_get auto_keybox)" = 1 ] || return 0
    url=$(conf_get keybox_url)
    if [ -z "$url" ]; then
        log_line 'auto_keybox=1 but keybox_url is empty: nothing fetched'
        return 0
    fi
    case $url in
        https://*) : ;;
        http://*) log_line "WARN: $url uses plain HTTP, so nothing on the path is authenticated" ;;
        *) log_line "WARN: $url is not an http(s) URL, refused"; return 0 ;;
    esac
    case $url in
        *' '*|*'"'*) log_line 'WARN: keybox_url contains a space or a quote, refused'; return 0 ;;
    esac
    want=$(conf_get keybox_sha256)
    if [ -z "$want" ]; then
        log_line 'WARN: no keybox_sha256 configured, so the served file is trusted as it arrives'
    fi
    if command -v curl >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        fetch() { curl -fsSL --max-time 30 -o "$1" "$2"; }
    elif command -v wget >/dev/null 2>&1; then
        fetch() { wget -q -O "$1" --timeout=30 "$2"; }
    else
        log_line 'WARN: neither curl nor wget is present: nothing fetched'
        return 0
    fi
    mkdir -p "$TS_DIR" 2>/dev/null
    tmp=$TS_DIR/.keybox.download
    if ! fetch "$tmp" "$url" 2>/dev/null; then
        log_line "fetch failed: $url"
        rm -f "$tmp" 2>/dev/null
        return 0
    fi
    got=$(sha256sum "$tmp" 2>/dev/null | cut -d' ' -f1)
    if [ -n "$want" ] && [ "$want" != "$got" ]; then
        log_line "sha256 mismatch: want $want got $got, keeping the current keybox"
        rm -f "$tmp" 2>/dev/null
        return 0
    fi
    if [ -f "$KEYBOX" ] && [ "$(sha256sum "$KEYBOX" 2>/dev/null | cut -d' ' -f1)" = "$got" ]; then
        log_line "keybox unchanged, sha256 $got"
        rm -f "$tmp" 2>/dev/null
        return 0
    fi
    mv "$tmp" "$KEYBOX"
    chmod 600 "$KEYBOX" 2>/dev/null
    log_line "keybox replaced from $url sha256=$got"
    return 0
}

keybox_apply_mode() {
    mode=$(conf_get keybox_mode)
    : "${mode:=auto}"
    case $mode in
        existing|auto|software) : ;;
        *)
            log_line "WARN: unknown keybox_mode=$mode, treating it as auto"
            mode=auto
            ;;
    esac
    if [ "$mode" = existing ]; then
        log_line 'keybox_mode=existing: the bundled software keybox is not installed'
        return 0
    fi
    if [ "$mode" = auto ] && [ -f "$KEYBOX" ]; then
        log_line "keeping the keybox already at $KEYBOX"
        return 0
    fi
    src=$MOD_DIR/software-keybox.xml
    if [ ! -f "$src" ]; then
        log_line "WARN: $src is missing, keeping the current keybox"
        return 0
    fi
    mkdir -p "$TS_DIR" 2>/dev/null
    if [ -f "$KEYBOX" ] && cmp -s "$src" "$KEYBOX"; then
        log_line 'the bundled software keybox is already in place'
        return 0
    fi
    if [ -f "$KEYBOX" ]; then
        cp -f "$KEYBOX" "$KEYBOX.bak.$(date +%Y%m%d%H%M%S)" 2>/dev/null
        log_line "backed up the keybox that was in use"
    fi
    cp -f "$src" "$KEYBOX"
    chmod 600 "$KEYBOX" 2>/dev/null
    log_line "installed the bundled software keybox (ecdsa+rsa)"
}

```

`root/integrity-module/module/bin/lib.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
# shellcheck disable=SC2034
INTEGRITY_ROOT=${INTEGRITY_ROOT:-}
TS_DIR=$INTEGRITY_ROOT/data/adb/tricky_store
MOD_DIR=$INTEGRITY_ROOT/data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix
CONF=$TS_DIR/integrity.conf
KEYBOX=$TS_DIR/keybox.xml
TARGETS_FILE=$TS_DIR/target.txt
SP_FILE=$TS_DIR/security_patch.txt
PIF_PROP=$MOD_DIR/pif.prop
LOG_FILE=$TS_DIR/integrity.log
CHECKER_PKG=com.henrikherzig.playintegritychecker

conf_get() {
    [ -f "$CONF" ] || return 0
    sed -n "s/^[[:space:]]*$1[[:space:]]*=[[:space:]]*//p" "$CONF" | tail -n 1 | tr -d '\r'
}

file_get() {
    [ -f "$1" ] || return 0
    sed -n "s/^[[:space:]]*$2[[:space:]]*=[[:space:]]*//p" "$1" | tail -n 1 | tr -d '\r'
}

file_set() {
    f=$1
    k=$2
    v=$3
    [ -f "$f" ] || return 1
    if grep -q "^[[:space:]]*${k}[[:space:]]*=" "$f"; then
        sed -i "s|^[[:space:]]*${k}[[:space:]]*=.*|$k=$v|" "$f"
    else
        printf '%s=%s\n' "$k" "$v" >> "$f"
    fi
}

log_line() {
    mkdir -p "$TS_DIR" 2>/dev/null
    printf '[%s] %s\n' "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')" "$*" >> "$LOG_FILE" 2>/dev/null
    printf '%s\n' "$*"
}

```

`root/integrity-module/module/bin/sp.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
bin_dir=$(dirname -- "$0")
: "${INTEGRITY_BIN:=$bin_dir}"
. "$INTEGRITY_BIN/lib.sh"

ym_next5() {
    yy=20$(printf '%s' "$1" | cut -c1-2)
    mm=$(printf '%s' "$1" | cut -c3-4 | sed 's/^0*//')
    mm=${mm:-0}
    if [ "$mm" -eq 12 ]; then
        yy=$((yy + 1))
        mm=1
    else
        mm=$((mm + 1))
    fi
    printf '%04d-%02d-05\n' "$yy" "$mm"
}

sp_show() {
    printf 'security_patch.txt:\n'
    if [ -f "$SP_FILE" ]; then
        sed 's/^/  /' "$SP_FILE"
    else
        printf '  (missing)\n'
    fi
    printf 'pif.prop SECURITY_PATCH: %s\n' "$(file_get "$PIF_PROP" SECURITY_PATCH)"
}

sp_set() {
    case ${1:-} in
        [0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]-[0-9][0-9]) : ;;
        *)
            printf 'usage: integrity.sh sp set YYYY-MM-DD\n' >&2
            return 2
            ;;
    esac
    mkdir -p "$TS_DIR"
    printf 'system=%s\nvendor=%s\nboot=%s\n' "$1" "$1" "$1" > "$SP_FILE"
    file_set "$PIF_PROP" SECURITY_PATCH "$1" || {
        printf 'WARN: cannot update %s\n' "$PIF_PROP" >&2
        return 1
    }
    printf 'security patch set to %s in %s and %s\n' "$1" "$SP_FILE" "$PIF_PROP"
}

sp_check() {
    rc=0
    sp=$(file_get "$PIF_PROP" SECURITY_PATCH)
    if [ -z "$sp" ]; then
        printf 'WARN: %s has no SECURITY_PATCH\n' "$PIF_PROP"
        return 1
    fi
    for k in system vendor boot; do
        v=$(sed -n "s/^$k=//p" "$SP_FILE" 2>/dev/null | tail -n 1)
        if [ -z "$v" ]; then
            printf 'WARN: %s has no %s= line\n' "$SP_FILE" "$k"
            rc=1
            continue
        fi
        if [ "$v" != "$sp" ] && [ "$(printf '%s' "$v" | tr -d '-')" != "$(printf '%s' "$sp" | tr -d '-')" ]; then
            printf 'WARN: %s=%s but pif.prop SECURITY_PATCH=%s\n' "$k" "$v" "$sp"
            rc=1
        fi
    done
    case $sp in
        *-05) : ;;
        *)
            printf 'WARN: SECURITY_PATCH %s does not sit on the 5th\n' "$sp"
            rc=1
            ;;
    esac
    fp=$(file_get "$PIF_PROP" FINGERPRINT)
    build=$(printf '%s' "$fp" | awk -F/ '{print $4}')
    stamp=$(printf '%s' "$build" | grep -oE '[0-9]{6}' | head -n 1)
    if [ -n "$stamp" ]; then
        want=$(ym_next5 "$stamp")
        if [ "$want" != "$sp" ]; then
            printf 'WARN: fingerprint build id %s implies %s, pif.prop says %s\n' "$build" "$want" "$sp"
            rc=1
        fi
    else
        printf 'WARN: cannot read a YYMMDD stamp from build id %s\n' "$build"
        rc=1
    fi
    return $rc
}

case ${1:-show} in
    show) sp_show ;;
    set) shift; sp_set "$@" ;;
    check) sp_check ;;
    next5) shift; ym_next5 "$@" ;;
    *)
        printf 'usage: sp.sh show|set <YYYY-MM-DD>|check|next5 <YYMMDD>\n' >&2
        exit 2
        ;;
esac

```

`root/integrity-module/module/bin/targets.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
bin_dir=$(dirname -- "$0")
: "${INTEGRITY_BIN:=$bin_dir}"
. "$INTEGRITY_BIN/lib.sh"

targets_default() {
    printf '%s\n' com.android.vending com.google.android.gms com.google.android.gsf "$CHECKER_PKG"
}

targets_required() {
    printf '%s\n' com.android.vending com.google.android.gms
}

targets_reset() {
    mkdir -p "$TS_DIR"
    targets_default > "$TARGETS_FILE"
    printf 'target list reset to %s packages\n' "$(targets_default | wc -l | tr -d ' ')"
}

targets_merge() {
    mkdir -p "$TS_DIR"
    [ -f "$TARGETS_FILE" ] || : > "$TARGETS_FILE"
    added=0
    for p in $(targets_default); do
        if grep -qxF "$p" "$TARGETS_FILE" 2>/dev/null; then
            continue
        fi
        printf '%s\n' "$p" >> "$TARGETS_FILE"
        added=$((added + 1))
    done
    printf 'target list: %s added, %s entries\n' "$added" "$(grep -cv '^#' "$TARGETS_FILE" | tr -d ' ')"
}

targets_add() {
    mkdir -p "$TS_DIR"
    [ -f "$TARGETS_FILE" ] || : > "$TARGETS_FILE"
    for p in "$@"; do
        if grep -qxF "$p" "$TARGETS_FILE" 2>/dev/null; then
            continue
        fi
        printf '%s\n' "$p" >> "$TARGETS_FILE"
    done
}

targets_rm() {
    [ -f "$TARGETS_FILE" ] || return 0
    for p in "$@"; do
        grep -vxF "$p" "$TARGETS_FILE" > "$TARGETS_FILE.tmp"
        mv "$TARGETS_FILE.tmp" "$TARGETS_FILE"
    done
}

targets_check() {
    rc=0
    for p in $(targets_required); do
        if ! grep -qxF "$p" "$TARGETS_FILE" 2>/dev/null; then
            printf 'WARN: %s missing from %s\n' "$p" "$TARGETS_FILE"
            rc=1
        fi
    done
    return $rc
}

case ${1:-list} in
    reset) targets_reset ;;
    merge) targets_merge ;;
    add) shift; targets_add "$@" ;;
    rm) shift; targets_rm "$@" ;;
    list) [ -f "$TARGETS_FILE" ] && cat "$TARGETS_FILE" ;;
    check) targets_check ;;
    *)
        printf 'usage: targets.sh reset|merge|add <pkg>...|rm <pkg>...|list|check\n' >&2
        exit 2
        ;;
esac

```

`root/integrity-module/module/bin/verdict-parse.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
raw=$(cat)
[ -n "$raw" ] || raw='{}'

dev=$(printf '%s' "$raw" | grep -o '"deviceRecognitionVerdict": \[[^]]*\]' | sed 's/.*\[//;s/\].*//;s/"//g;s/ //g')
app=$(printf '%s' "$raw" | grep -o '"appRecognitionVerdict": "[A-Z_]*"' | sed 's/.*"appRecognitionVerdict": "//;s/"$//')
lic=$(printf '%s' "$raw" | grep -o '"appLicensingVerdict": "[A-Z_]*"' | sed 's/.*"appLicensingVerdict": "//;s/"$//')
boot=$(cat /proc/sys/kernel/random/boot_id 2>/dev/null)

printf '{"ts":"%s","boot_id":"%s","device":"%s","app":"%s","licensing":"%s"}\n' \
    "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')" "${boot:-unknown}" "${dev:-none}" "${app:-none}" "${lic:-none}"

`root/integrity-module/module/customize.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
MODPATH="${MODPATH:-/data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix}"
TS_DIR=/data/adb/tricky_store
ui_print() { echo "$1"; }

set_perm() {
    chown "$2:$3" "$1" 2>/dev/null
    chmod "$4" "$1" 2>/dev/null
}

ui_print "- mblu21 integrity: TrickyStore + Play Integrity Fork"
mkdir -p "$TS_DIR"
LEGACY=$TS_DIR/legacy-$(date +%Y%m%d%H%M%S)

if [ -f /data/adb/modules/tricky_store/module.prop ]; then
    mkdir -p "$LEGACY"
    mv /data/adb/modules/tricky_store "$LEGACY/tricky_store" 2>/dev/null
    ui_print "- the module that held /data/adb/tricky_store moved to $LEGACY/tricky_store"
fi

for ART in alwaysstrong hbk boot_hash.bin boot_key.bin persistent_keys logs pif.prop; do
    [ -e "$TS_DIR/$ART" ] || continue
    mkdir -p "$LEGACY"
    mv "$TS_DIR/$ART" "$LEGACY/" 2>/dev/null
done

if [ -f "$TS_DIR/keybox.xml" ]; then
    ui_print "- keeping the keybox already at $TS_DIR/keybox.xml"
else
    ui_print "- no keybox: TrickyStore runs certificate-hack mode only"
fi

for f in integrity.conf target.txt security_patch.txt; do
    if [ -f "$TS_DIR/$f" ]; then
        ui_print "- keeping the existing $TS_DIR/$f"
    else
        cp -f "$MODPATH/$f" "$TS_DIR/$f"
    fi
done

sh "$MODPATH/bin/targets.sh" merge || ui_print "- target list merge failed"
INTEGRITY_BIN="$MODPATH/bin"
. "$INTEGRITY_BIN/keybox.sh"
keybox_apply_mode

find "$MODPATH" -type f -name '*.sh' -exec chmod 755 {} + 2>/dev/null
chmod 755 "$MODPATH/tricky/daemon" "$MODPATH/tricky/machikado.arm64" "$MODPATH/tricky/machikado.arm" "$MODPATH/tricky/machikado.x64" 2>/dev/null
chmod 644 "$MODPATH/pif.prop" "$MODPATH/tricky/service.apk" "$MODPATH/classes.dex" 2>/dev/null
for f in integrity.conf target.txt security_patch.txt; do
    set_perm "$TS_DIR/$f" 0 0 644
done
ui_print "- done; reboot, then: adb shell sh $MODPATH/bin/integrity.sh doctor"

```

`root/integrity-module/module/integrity.conf`:

```conf
keybox_mode=auto
auto_keybox=0
keybox_url=
keybox_sha256=
ts_daemon=0
auto_verdict=1
keep_adb=1
spic_tap_x=360
spic_tap_y=594

```

`root/integrity-module/module/licenses/NOTICE.md`:

```md
# Third-party notices

The installable zip is assembled from the official release artifacts below. Nothing is
fetched at boot unless `auto_keybox=1` is set in `/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf`.

| Component | Version | Source | sha256 |
|---|---|---|---|
| Tricky Store | 1.4.1 (245-72b2e84) | `https://github.com/5ec1cff/TrickyStore/releases/download/1.4.1/Tricky-Store-v1.4.1-245-72b2e84-release.zip` | `2f5e73fcba0e4e43b6e96b38f333cbe394873e3a81cf8fe1b831c2fbd6c46ea9` |
| Play Integrity Fork | v19 | `https://github.com/osm0sis/PlayIntegrityFork/releases/download/v19/PlayIntegrityFork-v19.zip` | `83b4d5f32743805c5b5a69361d73b8ab9c095325e24f20711afa1abea837b58f` |

## Tricky Store

Upstream publishes no license file, so no license is granted here beyond the upstream
terms. The artifact is pulled from its official release at build time and is not vendored
in this repository. Redistributing the assembled zip outside the owner's own device is
not granted by this project. Authors: 5ec1cff, aviraxp, Cyberenchanter.

## Play Integrity Fork

GPL-3.0. The full text is `GPL-3.0.txt` in this directory. Copyright osm0sis and
chiteroman @ xda-developers.

## What this module changes in the upstream payloads

* Play Integrity Fork: `post-fs-data.sh` and `service.sh` are shipped as
  `pif-post-fs-data.sh` and `pif-service.sh` and sourced by this module's own scripts.
  Their content is unmodified. `pif.prop` is replaced by this module's default and is
  kept in `/data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/pif.prop`, where the upstream companion
  library looks for it.
* Tricky Store: `service.apk`, `daemon`, `libtricky_store.so` (arm64), `machikado`,
  `mazoku` and `sepolicy.rule` are unmodified and keep their upstream layout at the
  module root. `build-module.sh` checks each of them against the `.sha256` file that ships
  in the official zip before it is copied. The daemon is started from this module's
  `service.sh`.
* Tricky Store's release zip also carries a default software keybox. It is shipped here as
  `software-keybox.xml` and is only installed when `keybox_mode=software`, or when no
  keybox exists yet. It is a self-signed test chain that Google does not sign.
* The module id stays `playintegrityfix` on purpose: the upstream companion library opens
  `/data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/classes.dex` and `.../pif.prop` by absolute path.

```

`root/integrity-module/module/service.sh`:

```sh
#!/system/bin/sh
PATH=/data/adb/ksu/bin:/data/adb/ap/bin:/data/adb/magisk:$PATH
export PATH
ROOT=${INTEGRITY_ROOT:-}
MODPATH="${0%/*}"
TS_DIR=$ROOT/data/adb/tricky_store
mkdir -p "$TS_DIR" 2>/dev/null

bootlog() {
    printf '[%s] %s\n' "$(date '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S')" "$*" >> "$TS_DIR/integrity.log" 2>/dev/null
}

bootlog "service.sh start, module $MODPATH"

if [ -f "$MODPATH/pif-service.sh" ]; then
    . "$MODPATH/pif-service.sh"
    bootlog "pif service stage done"
fi

INTEGRITY_BIN="$MODPATH/bin"
if [ -f "$INTEGRITY_BIN/keybox.sh" ]; then
    . "$INTEGRITY_BIN/keybox.sh"
    keybox_auto_apply
fi

sh "$MODPATH/bin/fix-persist.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1

chmod 755 "$MODPATH/daemon" "$MODPATH/machikado" "$MODPATH/mazoku" 2>/dev/null
chmod 644 "$MODPATH/libtricky_store.so" "$MODPATH/service.apk" 2>/dev/null

ts_wanted=$(conf_get ts_daemon)
if [ "$ts_wanted" = 1 ] && [ -x "$MODPATH/daemon" ]; then
    bootlog "ts_daemon=1: starting the TrickyStore loop with a short-lived backoff"
    (
        cd "$MODPATH" || exit 1
        short=0
        while :; do
            started=$(date +%s)
            ./daemon
            finished=$(date +%s)
            if [ $((finished - started)) -lt 10 ]; then
                short=$((short + 1))
            else
                short=0
            fi
            if [ "$short" -ge 5 ]; then
                bootlog "ts_daemon: stopped after five short-lived runs under 10 s"
                break
            fi
            sleep 2
        done
    ) &
else
    bootlog "ts_daemon=$ts_wanted: the TrickyStore loop is not started"
fi

if [ -x "$MODPATH/bin/boot-verdict.sh" ]; then
    nohup sh "$MODPATH/bin/boot-verdict.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1 &
fi

```

`root/integrity-module/tests/run.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
here=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
mod=$here/../module
export INTEGRITY_BIN=$mod/bin
pass=0
fail=0

ok() { printf 'ok   %s\n' "$1"; pass=$((pass + 1)); }
no() { printf 'FAIL %s\n' "$1"; fail=$((fail + 1)); }

fresh() {
    T=$(mktemp -d)
    export INTEGRITY_ROOT=$T
    mkdir -p "$T/data/adb/tricky_store" "$T/data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix" "$T/bin"
    cp "$mod/integrity.conf" "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf"
    cp "$mod/target.txt" "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/target.txt"
    cp "$mod/security_patch.txt" "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/security_patch.txt"
    cp "$mod/pif.prop" "$T/data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/pif.prop"
}

targets() { INTEGRITY_ROOT=$INTEGRITY_ROOT sh "$mod/bin/targets.sh" "$@"; }
sp() { INTEGRITY_ROOT=$INTEGRITY_ROOT sh "$mod/bin/sp.sh" "$@"; }

fresh
targets reset >/dev/null
n=$(wc -l < "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/target.txt")
[ "$n" -eq 4 ] && ok "targets reset writes 4 packages" || no "targets reset writes 4 packages (got $n)"
targets check >/dev/null && ok "default target list passes check" || no "default target list passes check"
targets rm com.google.android.gms >/dev/null
if targets check >/dev/null; then no "check fails when play services is missing"; else ok "check fails when play services is missing"; fi
targets add com.google.android.gms >/dev/null
targets add com.google.android.gms >/dev/null
c=$(grep -cxF com.google.android.gms "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/target.txt")
[ "$c" -eq 1 ] && ok "targets add is idempotent" || no "targets add is idempotent (got $c)"

fresh
printf 'auto_keybox=0\nkeybox_url=http://example.invalid/kb.xml\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf"
mkdir -p "$T/bin"
printf '#!/bin/sh\ntouch %s/curl-called\n' "$T" > "$T/bin/curl"
chmod 755 "$T/bin/curl"
( export PATH=$T/bin:$PATH; . "$mod/bin/keybox.sh"; keybox_auto_apply >/dev/null 2>&1 )
[ -e "$T/curl-called" ] && no "auto_keybox=0 never fetches" || ok "auto_keybox=0 never fetches"

printf 'auto_keybox=1\nkeybox_url=\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf"
( export PATH=$T/bin:$PATH; . "$mod/bin/keybox.sh"; keybox_auto_apply >/dev/null 2>&1 )
[ -e "$T/curl-called" ] && no "empty keybox_url never fetches" || ok "empty keybox_url never fetches"
grep -q 'keybox_url is empty' "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.log" && ok "empty keybox_url is logged" || no "empty keybox_url is logged"

fresh
printf 'auto_keybox=1\nkeybox_url=http://example.invalid/kb.xml\nkeybox_sha256=deadbeef\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf"
printf '#!/bin/sh\nwhile [ $# -gt 0 ]; do if [ "$1" = -o ]; then shift; printf "<xml/>" > "$1"; fi; shift; done\n' > "$T/bin/curl"
chmod 755 "$T/bin/curl"
( export PATH=$T/bin:$PATH; . "$mod/bin/keybox.sh"; keybox_auto_apply >/dev/null 2>&1 )
[ -e "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/keybox.xml" ] && no "sha256 mismatch keeps the old keybox" || ok "sha256 mismatch keeps the old keybox"
grep -q 'sha256 mismatch' "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.log" && ok "sha256 mismatch is logged" || no "sha256 mismatch is logged"
grep -q 'plain HTTP' "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.log" && ok "http source is called out" || no "http source is called out"

fresh
printf '<Key algorithm="ecdsa">\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/keybox.xml"
out=$(. "$mod/bin/keybox.sh"; keybox_report)
printf '%s' "$out" | grep -q 'no rsa key' && ok "ecdsa-only keybox is called incomplete" || no "ecdsa-only keybox is called incomplete"
rm -f "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/keybox.xml"
out=$(. "$mod/bin/keybox.sh"; keybox_report)
printf '%s' "$out" | grep -q 'certificate-hack mode' && ok "missing keybox reports certificate-hack mode" || no "missing keybox reports certificate-hack mode"
printf '<Key algorithm="ecdsa">\n<Key algorithm="rsa">\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/keybox.xml"
out=$(. "$mod/bin/keybox.sh"; keybox_report)
printf '%s' "$out" | grep -q 'complete' && ok "ecdsa+rsa keybox is complete" || no "ecdsa+rsa keybox is complete"

[ "$(sp next5 260918)" = 2026-10-05 ] && ok "next5 260918 -> 2026-10-05" || no "next5 260918 -> 2026-10-05"
[ "$(sp next5 261231)" = 2027-01-05 ] && ok "next5 261231 rolls into 2027" || no "next5 261231 rolls into 2027"

fresh
sp check >/dev/null && ok "shipped security patch matches the fingerprint build id" || no "shipped security patch matches the fingerprint build id"
sed -i 's|^FINGERPRINT=.*|FINGERPRINT=google/bluejay_beta/bluejay:CANARY/ZP11.260918.007/16484274:user/release-keys|' "$T/data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/pif.prop"
sp set 2026-10-05 >/dev/null
grep -qx 'system=2026-10-05' "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/security_patch.txt" && ok "sp set rewrites security_patch.txt" || no "sp set rewrites security_patch.txt"
grep -qx 'SECURITY_PATCH=2026-10-05' "$T/data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/pif.prop" && ok "sp set rewrites pif.prop" || no "sp set rewrites pif.prop"
sp check >/dev/null && ok "sp check accepts the aligned pair" || no "sp check accepts the aligned pair"
sp set 2026-06-05 >/dev/null
if sp check >/dev/null; then no "sp check rejects a rom-aligned date"; else ok "sp check rejects a rom-aligned date"; fi

fresh
printf 'system=2026-11-05\nvendor=2024-12-05\nboot=2026-11-05\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/security_patch.txt"
if out=$(sp check 2>&1); then no "sp check catches a divergent vendor line"; else ok "sp check catches a divergent vendor line"; fi
printf '%s' "$out" | grep -q 'vendor=' && ok "divergent vendor line is named" || no "divergent vendor line is named"

fresh
mkdir -p "$T/data/property"
printf 'x' > "$T/data/property/persistent_properties"
chmod 0666 "$T/data/property/persistent_properties"
sh "$mod/post-fs-data.sh"
[ "$(stat -c %a "$T/data/property/persistent_properties")" = 600 ] && ok "post-fs-data restores persistent_properties to 0600" || no "post-fs-data restores persistent_properties to 0600"

fresh
mkdir -p "$T/data/property"
printf 'x' > "$T/data/property/persistent_properties"
chmod 0666 "$T/data/property/persistent_properties"
printf '#!/bin/sh\necho\n' > "$T/bin/getprop"
printf '#!/bin/sh\nprintf "%%s\\n" "$*" > %s/setprop-args\n' "$T" > "$T/bin/setprop"
printf '#!/bin/sh\nprintf y > %s/pp.new; chmod 666 %s/pp.new; mv %s/pp.new %s/data/property/persistent_properties\n' "$T" "$T" "$T" "$T" > "$T/bin/resetprop"
chmod 755 "$T/bin/getprop" "$T/bin/setprop" "$T/bin/resetprop"
( export PATH=$T/bin:$PATH; sh "$mod/bin/fix-persist.sh" >/dev/null )
[ "$(stat -c %a "$T/data/property/persistent_properties")" = 600 ] && ok "re-arming adb leaves persistent_properties at 0600" || no "re-arming adb leaves persistent_properties at 0600"
grep -qx 'persist.sys.usb.config adb' "$T/setprop-args" 2>/dev/null && ok "lost adb config is re-armed through init" || no "lost adb config is re-armed through init"
fresh
cat > "$T/fixture.log" <<'EOF'
I/System.out( 8414): {
I/System.out( 8414):   "appIntegrity": {
I/System.out( 8414):     "appRecognitionVerdict": "PLAY_RECOGNIZED",
I/System.out( 8414):   },
I/System.out( 8414):   "deviceIntegrity": {
I/System.out( 8414):     "deviceRecognitionVerdict": ["MEETS_BASIC_INTEGRITY", "MEETS_DEVICE_INTEGRITY"],
I/System.out( 8414):   },
I/System.out( 8414):   "accountDetails": {
I/System.out( 8414):     "appLicensingVerdict": "LICENSED"
I/System.out( 8414):   },
I/System.out( 8414): }
EOF
line=$(sh "$mod/bin/verdict-parse.sh" < "$T/fixture.log")
printf '%s' "$line" | grep -q '"device":"MEETS_BASIC_INTEGRITY,MEETS_DEVICE_INTEGRITY"' && ok "verdict-parse keeps every device verdict" || no "verdict-parse keeps every device verdict (got $line)"
printf '%s' "$line" | grep -q '"app":"PLAY_RECOGNIZED"' && ok "verdict-parse reads the app verdict" || no "verdict-parse reads the app verdict"
printf '%s' "$line" | grep -q '"licensing":"LICENSED"' && ok "verdict-parse reads the licensing verdict" || no "verdict-parse reads the licensing verdict"
line=$(printf 'nothing useful
' | sh "$mod/bin/verdict-parse.sh")
printf '%s' "$line" | grep -q '"device":"none"' && ok "verdict-parse marks a missing verdict" || no "verdict-parse marks a missing verdict"

fresh
printf 'keybox_mode=auto
auto_verdict=0
' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf"
rm -f "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/verdicts.jsonl"
printf '#!/bin/sh\n[ "$1" = sys.boot_completed ] && echo 1\n' > "$T/bin/getprop"
for stub in sleep am input wm logcat; do printf '#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n' > "$T/bin/$stub"; done
printf '#!/bin/sh\necho "  mCurrentFocus=Window{abc u0 com.henrikherzig.playintegritychecker/x}"\n' > "$T/bin/dumpsys"
chmod +x "$T/bin/"*
INTEGRITY_BIN=$mod/bin INTEGRITY_ROOT=$T PATH=$T/bin:$PATH sh "$mod/bin/boot-verdict.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1
[ -f "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/verdicts.jsonl" ] && no "auto_verdict=0 records nothing" || ok "auto_verdict=0 records nothing"


fresh
printf 'keybox_mode=software\nauto_keybox=0\nts_daemon=0\nauto_verdict=1\nkeep_adb=1\nverdict_delay=0\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf"
printf '<Key algorithm="ecdsa">\n<Key algorithm="rsa">\n' > "$T/data/adb/modules/playintegrityfix/software-keybox.xml"
printf '<Key algorithm="ecdsa">\n<Key algorithm="rsa">\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/keybox.xml"
n1=$( (ls "$T/data/adb/tricky_store"/keybox.xml.bak.* 2>/dev/null || true) | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
INTEGRITY_BIN=$mod/bin INTEGRITY_ROOT=$T sh -c '. "$INTEGRITY_BIN/keybox.sh"; keybox_apply_mode' >/dev/null 2>&1
n2=$( (ls "$T/data/adb/tricky_store"/keybox.xml.bak.* 2>/dev/null || true) | wc -l | tr -d ' ')
[ "$n1" = "$n2" ] && ok "an identical software keybox makes no new backup" || no "an identical software keybox makes no new backup ($n1 -> $n2)"

fresh
printf '#!/bin/sh\n[ "$1" = sys.boot_completed ] && echo 1\n' > "$T/bin/getprop"
printf '#!/bin/sh\necho "setprop $*" >> "%s/setprop.trace"\n' "$T" > "$T/bin/setprop"
printf '#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n' > "$T/bin/sleep"
printf '#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n' > "$T/bin/resetprop"
chmod +x "$T/bin/"*
printf 'keep_adb=0\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf"
mkdir -p "$T/data/property"
printf 'x' > "$T/data/property/persistent_properties"
chmod 0666 "$T/data/property/persistent_properties"
INTEGRITY_ROOT=$T PATH=$T/bin:$PATH sh "$mod/bin/fix-persist.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1
grep -q 'usb.config' "$T/setprop.trace" 2>/dev/null && no "keep_adb=0 leaves usb debugging alone" || ok "keep_adb=0 leaves usb debugging alone"
[ "$(stat -c %a "$T/data/property/persistent_properties")" = 600 ] && ok "fix-persist still repairs the mode with keep_adb=0" || no "fix-persist still repairs the mode with keep_adb=0"
printf 'keep_adb=1\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf"
chmod 0666 "$T/data/property/persistent_properties"
INTEGRITY_ROOT=$T PATH=$T/bin:$PATH sh "$mod/bin/fix-persist.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1
grep -q 'setprop persist.sys.usb.config adb' "$T/setprop.trace" 2>/dev/null && ok "keep_adb=1 re-arms adb" || no "keep_adb=1 re-arms adb"

fresh
printf 'keybox_mode=auto\nts_daemon=0\nauto_verdict=0\nkeep_adb=1\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf"
INTEGRITY_ROOT=$T PATH=$T/bin:$PATH sh "$mod/service.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1
grep -q 'ts_daemon=0: the TrickyStore loop is not started' "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.log" 2>/dev/null && ok "service.sh does not start the daemon loop by default" || no "service.sh does not start the daemon loop by default"

fresh
cp -a "$mod" "$T/mod"
printf '#!/bin/sh\necho run >> "%s/daemon.count"\n' "$T" > "$T/mod/daemon"
chmod 755 "$T/mod/daemon"
printf 'keybox_mode=auto\nts_daemon=1\nauto_verdict=0\nkeep_adb=1\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf"
printf '#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n' > "$T/bin/sleep"
chmod +x "$T/bin/sleep"
INTEGRITY_ROOT=$T PATH=$T/bin:$PATH sh "$T/mod/service.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1
sleep 2
c1=$(wc -l < "$T/daemon.count" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' ')
command sleep 2
c2=$(wc -l < "$T/daemon.count" 2>/dev/null | tr -d ' ')
[ "$c1" = 5 ] && [ "$c2" = 5 ] && ok "the daemon loop runs exactly five short-lived times, then stops" || no "the daemon loop runs exactly five short-lived times, then stops (got $c1 then $c2)"
pkill -f "$T/mod/service.sh" 2>/dev/null || true

fresh
printf '#!/bin/sh\n[ "$1" = sys.boot_completed ] && echo 1\n' > "$T/bin/getprop"
printf '#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n' > "$T/bin/sleep"
printf '#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n' > "$T/bin/am"
printf '#!/bin/sh\necho "input $*" >> "%s/input.trace"\n' "$T" > "$T/bin/input"
printf '#!/bin/sh\nexit 0\n' > "$T/bin/wm"
printf '#!/bin/sh\necho "  mCurrentFocus=Window{abc u0 NotificationShade}"\n' > "$T/bin/dumpsys"
printf '#!/bin/sh\ncat "%s/fixture.log" 2>/dev/null\n' "$T" > "$T/bin/logcat"
chmod +x "$T/bin/"*
printf 'keybox_mode=auto\nauto_verdict=1\nverdict_delay=0\n' > "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/integrity.conf"
INTEGRITY_ROOT=$T PATH=$T/bin:$PATH sh "$mod/bin/boot-verdict.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1
grep -q 'skipped:NotificationShade' "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/verdicts.jsonl" 2>/dev/null && ok "boot-verdict records a skip when another window has focus" || no "boot-verdict records a skip when another window has focus"
grep -q 'input tap' "$T/input.trace" 2>/dev/null && no "boot-verdict does not tap without focus" || ok "boot-verdict does not tap without focus"

printf '#!/bin/sh\necho "  mCurrentFocus=Window{abc u0 com.henrikherzig.playintegritychecker/com.henrikherzig.playintegritychecker.MainActivity}"\n' > "$T/bin/dumpsys"
chmod +x "$T/bin/dumpsys"
: > "$T/input.trace"
printf 'I/System.out( 1): {\nI/System.out( 1):   "appIntegrity": { "appRecognitionVerdict": "PLAY_RECOGNIZED" },\nI/System.out( 1):   "deviceIntegrity": { "deviceRecognitionVerdict": ["MEETS_BASIC_INTEGRITY"] },\nI/System.out( 1):   "accountDetails": { "appLicensingVerdict": "LICENSED" } }\n' > "$T/fixture.log"
INTEGRITY_ROOT=$T PATH=$T/bin:$PATH sh "$mod/bin/boot-verdict.sh" >/dev/null 2>&1
grep -q 'input tap 360 594' "$T/input.trace" 2>/dev/null && ok "boot-verdict taps once the checker has focus" || no "boot-verdict taps once the checker has focus"
grep -q '"device":"MEETS_BASIC_INTEGRITY"' "$T/data/adb/tricky_store/verdicts.jsonl" 2>/dev/null && ok "boot-verdict records the parsed verdict when focused" || no "boot-verdict records the parsed verdict when focused"

printf '
%s passed, %s failed
' "$pass" "$fail"
[ "$fail" -eq 0 ]

`root/susfs-guard-module/README.md`:

```md
# SUSFS guard module

KernelSU module that loads `susfs_guard_lkm.ko` at boot, so SUSFS works on a phone rooted through `init_boot` (the LKM path) without a rebuilt kernel.

| File | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `module.prop` | module identity: id `susfs_guard_lkm`, version `v2.3.0-gki` |
| `post-fs-data.sh`, `service.sh` | both call `/data/adb/ksud insmod /data/adb/modules/susfs_guard_lkm/susfs_guard_lkm.ko` and return early when `/sys/module/susfs_guard_lkm` already exists |
| `build-module.sh` | rebuilds the installable zip from the files above plus a `susfs_guard_lkm.ko` |

The module binary is not vendored here. It is the KernelSU-Next SUSFS guard for `android13-5.15`, published in the `mblu21-root-20261102` release:

| Artifact | sha256 |
|---|---|
| `susfs_guard_lkm.ko` | `024aa0de96b9e2d45c7f9a086189d72f95d51028fec6621b48e5f548cd39dc26` |
| `susfs_guard_lkm-module.zip` (as published) | `55740001f1133f067b324a316c779fe8907275d120dfc94cb9d8cef359ac9f66` |

Rebuilding produces a zip whose own sha256 differs, because zip stores file timestamps; every entry inside is byte-identical to the published zip (`module.prop` `61e9e9a5…`, `post-fs-data.sh` and `service.sh` `93419214…`, `susfs_guard_lkm.ko` `024aa0de…`). Compare entry hashes, not the container.

## Use

```sh
adb push susfs_guard_lkm-module.zip /data/local/tmp/
adb shell su -c 'ksud module install /data/local/tmp/susfs_guard_lkm-module.zip'
adb reboot
adb shell su -c 'ksud susfs implementation'   # lkm
adb shell su -c 'cat /data/adb/ksu/log/susfs_guard.log'
```

The log must end with `rc=0 present=yes` for the boot that just happened.

## Rebuild

```sh
./build-module.sh susfs_guard_lkm-module.zip /path/to/susfs_guard_lkm.ko
```

Works on the stock `5.15.149` kernel of this phone. It loads even though the module's vermagic says `5.15.202`, because `ksud insmod` loads with kallsyms access and skips the module version check, and the guard resolves SukiSU's symbols by name rather than by CRC.

```

`root/susfs-guard-module/build-module.sh`:

```sh
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail
here=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")" && pwd)
out=${1:-susfs_guard_lkm-module.zip}
ko=${2:-$here/susfs_guard_lkm.ko}
if [ ! -f "$ko" ]; then
  echo "missing $ko: download susfs_guard_lkm.ko from the release page" >&2
  exit 1
fi
tmp=$(mktemp -d)
trap 'rm -rf "$tmp"' EXIT
install -m 644 "$here/module.prop" "$tmp/module.prop"
install -m 755 "$here/post-fs-data.sh" "$tmp/post-fs-data.sh"
install -m 755 "$here/service.sh" "$tmp/service.sh"
install -m 644 "$ko" "$tmp/susfs_guard_lkm.ko"
(
  cd "$tmp"
  zip -q -X "$out" module.prop post-fs-data.sh service.sh susfs_guard_lkm.ko
)
sha256sum "$out"

```

#!/system/bin/sh
LOG=/data/adb/ksu/log/susfs_guard.log
mkdir -p /data/adb/ksu/log
[ -e /sys/module/susfs_guard_lkm ] && exit 0
KO=/data/adb/modules/susfs_guard_lkm/susfs_guard_lkm.ko
echo "$(date '+%F %T') loading $KO" >> "$LOG"
/data/adb/ksud insmod "$KO" >> "$LOG" 2>&1
echo "$(date '+%F %T') rc=$? present=$([ -e /sys/module/susfs_guard_lkm ] && echo yes || echo no)" >> "$LOG"
